MA	<p>f8a1_d1e8</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>SgfI</i></span> is derived from Streptomyces griseoruber, and <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>ZraI</i></span> is derived from Zoogloea ramigera 11.
